## `AFG-4220-ENG-R3-ENG-SC-01`

- **Country:** `AFG` · **Track:** `ENG`
- **Role:** Pipeline-Weld-DR Inspector
- **Scenario:** Steady-State Process Parameter Micro-Tuning & Drift Compensation Calibration SOP for Buried Pipe Anti-Corrosion Coating Spark & Weld Radiographic RT Interpreter
- **Intro:** Pipeline-Weld-DR Inspector — Steady-State Process Parameter Micro-Tuning & Drift Compensation Calibration SOP for Buried Pipe Anti-Corrosion Coating Spark & Weld Radiographic RT Interpreter — Pre-Operation Safety and Radiation Zone Verification: O2: 19.5-23.5% by volume; H2S: <10 ppm; LEL: <10% of lower explosive limit; radiation dose rate at boundary < 2.5 mSv/h
- **Key tolerance:** O2: 19.5-23.5% by volume; H2S: <10 ppm; LEL: <10% of lower explosive limit; radiation dose rate at boundary < 2.5 mSv/h
- **Standards:** `ISO 9712:2012`, `ISO 17636-1:2013`, `ISO 14654:1999`, `ASTM G62-14 (Spark testing methods for holiday detection)`
